# FIV-CONN-04-D Post-Commit Integrity Reconciliation

**Document type:** Additive post-commit integrity reconciliation (not a PO decision, not an Independent Review, not an authorization, not a closure amendment).

**Date:** 2026-10-30

**Wave:** 6 — Live Trading / FIV-CONN-04-D

**Authority:** Prepared under Project Owner direction after post-commit audit verdict `PASS WITH OBSERVATIONS` on commit `4f8035daf1bfbacee7d232538d83a66c4d452a8f`.

**Revision:** Completeness reconciliation pass — occurrence-level accounting of all **328** unique seal-like `(source, line, hex)` candidates (resolves prior 197-row path-bound inventory gap).

---

## 1. Purpose and scope

This document:

1. Publishes **authoritative SHA-256 digests of the committed bytes** for the 87-path FIV-CONN-04-D package at the governing commit.
2. Inventories integrity-seal-like SHA-256 references embedded inside that package.
3. Distinguishes **historical / recording-time seals** from **valid current seals of committed bytes**.
4. Provides a **complete occurrence-level accounting** that reconciles to every unique seal-like `(source, line, hex)` candidate.
5. Confirms that substantive governance decisions, verdicts, and authorization boundaries remain intact.

This document does **not**:

- amend, replace, or re-decide any prior PO decision, IR, authorization, or closure act;
- rewrite historical hash values inside prior documents;
- authorize Stage 4+, implementation beyond the already-committed Stage 3 scaffold, deployment, production activation, or live-capital activity;
- create a new Git commit or modify the governing commit.

## 3. Automatic Prettier execution

Before the governing commit was finalized, the repository `lint-staged` / pre-commit hook ran `prettier --write` on the staged set. Prettier rewrote file bytes (presentation/formatting). Those rewritten bytes are what Git stored. Integrity seals recorded earlier against pre-hook working-tree bytes therefore often **do not equal** the digests of the committed blobs.

**Important:** A historical seal may correctly identify the pre-hook version that existed when a decision or review was recorded. This reconciliation does **not** claim those historical hashes were wrong at recording time. It states only that they are **not valid current seals** of the committed blobs when the digests differ.

Where a digest differs from committed bytes, this document does **not** assert a unique root cause (Prettier-only vs later substantive edit) unless Git evidence establishes it. For the **80 NEW** paths, no `HEAD^` parent exists, so pre-hook bytes are not recoverable from Git.

---

## 4. Method

1. Confirmed `HEAD == 4f8035daf1bfbacee7d232538d83a66c4d452a8f`, branch `main`, empty index, no push/fetch/pull for this task.
2. Listed package paths via `git diff-tree --no-commit-id --name-only -r HEAD` (87 paths).
3. For each path, computed `SHA-256(git cat-file -p HEAD:<path>)` — committed blob bytes, not dirty working-tree bytes.
4. Enumerated every unique `(source, line, hex)` 64-hex token in package text, then retained **seal-like** candidates: backtick-wrapped seals and/or tokens whose nearby window contains SHA-256 / digest / integrity / hash context (code files require an explicit SHA word).
5. For **each** of the **328** seal-like unique occurrences, attempted **textual** target binding only (same-line Markdown links / `docs|apps` paths / unique `.md` basenames; limited ±1-line context; explicit labels). **Hash-equality alone was not used to invent targets.**
6. Classified each occurrence into exactly one mutually exclusive category:
   - **MATCH** — recorded digest equals committed/HEAD bytes of a textually bound target;
   - **MISMATCH** — source presents verification language such as `MATCH` / `Verified` / `identical`, but recorded digest ≠ committed bytes;
   - **HISTORICAL** — integrity seal with text-bound target; digest ≠ committed bytes (recording-time / inventory / baseline);
   - **UNRESOLVED** — integrity/hash reference without a reliably text-bound artifact path;
   - **EXCLUDED — NOT AN INTEGRITY REFERENCE** — retained only if a seal-like candidate is later judged not to be an integrity reference (with explicit reason).
7. Separately recorded 64-hex tokens that failed the seal-like gate (not part of the 328).

## 6. Complete occurrence accounting (328)

### 6.1 Prior gap explained

The first reconciliation edition reported two figures that must not be subtracted from each other because they use **different granularities**:

- **328** = unique seal-like `(source, line, hex)` keys (SLH) — the complete candidate universe for this pass;
- **197** = prior classification rows at `(source, line, hex, target)` granularity (SLT) — incomplete extractor output, not a partition of the 328;
- those **197** SLT rows cover only **171** distinct SLH keys;
- therefore the **197** SLT rows contain **26** additional rows (`197 − 171 = 26`) that re-represent SLH keys already counted among the 171 (extra target bindings / duplicate SLT rows for the same SLH key);
- **157** SLH keys (`328 − 171 = 157`) were **absent** from the previous inventory — this is the true prior coverage gap;
- the **41** prior UNRESOLVED rows were **already included in the 197** SLT rows and must **not** be subtracted again from any remainder.

Status totals in that prior edition were MATCH=1, MISMATCH=31, HISTORICAL=124, UNRESOLVED=41 (sum **197** SLT rows).

**Do not treat `328 − 197` as an occurrence count.** Those operands are not commensurate (SLH keys vs SLT rows). Any remainder formed that way (including further subtracting the 41 UNRESOLVED to obtain “90”) is not a valid unexplained-occurrence tally.

| Quantity                                                  | Unit                                      | Count   | Role                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| Seal-like candidates                                      | unique `(source, line, hex)`              | **328** | Complete candidate universe for this pass            |
| Prior classified inventory                                | unique `(source, line, hex, target)` rows | **197** | Incomplete extractor output (not a partition of 328) |
| Distinct SLH keys covered by those 197 rows               | unique `(source, line, hex)`              | **171** | Overlap with the 328                                 |
| Extra SLT rows among the 197 for already-covered SLH keys | SLT rows                                  | **26**  | `197 − 171`; same SLH key, additional target/row     |
| SLH keys absent from the 197-row inventory                | unique `(source, line, hex)`              | **157** | True prior coverage gap (`328 − 171`)                |

Breakdown of the **157** previously uncovered SLH occurrences under this completeness pass:

- **HISTORICAL:** 69
- **MISMATCH:** 25
- **UNRESOLVED:** 63
- **Sum:** 157 (`69 + 25 + 63`)

### 6.2 Mutually exclusive totals for all 328

| Category                                  | Count   | Definition                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **MATCH**                                 | **1**   | Text-bound target; recorded digest equals committed/HEAD bytes                      |
| **MISMATCH**                              | **62**  | Verification language (`MATCH`/`Verified`/`identical`) but digest ≠ committed bytes |
| **HISTORICAL**                            | **181** | Integrity seal with text-bound target; digest ≠ committed bytes                     |
| **UNRESOLVED**                            | **84**  | Integrity/hash reference; no reliable textual target binding                        |
| **EXCLUDED — NOT AN INTEGRITY REFERENCE** | **0**   | Seal-like candidate judged not an integrity reference after occurrence review       |
| **TOTAL**                                 | **328** | Must equal **328**                                                                  |

**Reconciliation:** `1 + 62 + 181 + 84 + 0 = 328`.

### 6.4 Differentiation: historical vs current

| Kind                         | Meaning                                                                     | Use as current seal of committed bytes?           |
| ---------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| **Current committed digest** | SHA-256 of `HEAD:<path>` blob (Appendix A)                                  | **YES** — authoritative for this commit           |
| **MATCH reference**          | Embedded seal equals committed digest                                       | **YES** for that target                           |
| **HISTORICAL reference**     | Recording-time / inventory / baseline seal                                  | **NO**                                            |
| **MISMATCH reference**       | Document claimed verification MATCH against a digest that ≠ committed bytes | **NO** — treat recorded digest as historical only |
| **UNRESOLVED reference**     | Cannot reliably bind target from text                                       | **NO**                                            |
| **EXCLUDED**                 | Not an integrity reference                                                  | **NO**                                            |

## 12. Governance consistency findings

Read-only inspection of committed governance artifacts found **no substantive decision contradiction**.

### Stage 3 commencement

- Decision ID **`D-CONN04-FULL-D-IA-S23-B-S3`** remains intact.
- Bounded Stage 3 scope **S3-01…S3-06** remains the authorized inventory.
- Restrictions remain: Stages 4–8, Vault metadata port, `Connection.environment` UPDATE, Full-D backfill execution, FIV-CONN-04-E, production activation, and live-capital activity are **NOT AUTHORIZED**.

### Independent reviews

- Stage 2 IR verdict remains **PASS WITH OBSERVATIONS**.
- Stage 3 IR verdict remains **PASS WITH OBSERVATIONS**.
- **S3-OBS-01…S3-OBS-03** remain recorded observations (not silently closed).
- **F-OBS-01…F-OBS-04** remain **OPEN**.

### Stage 3 closure

- Decision ID **`D-CONN04-FULL-D-IA-S23-B-S3-CLOSE`** remains intact.
- Stage 3 remains **FORMALLY CLOSED**.
- Stage 4 commencement remains **NOT AUTHORIZED**.
- Stages 5–8 remain **NOT AUTHORIZED**.
- Documents state **NO AUTOMATIC PROGRESSION** to subsequent stages.
- Production activation, deployment, and live-capital activity remain **NOT AUTHORIZED**.

### Decision Register

- Stage 3 closure entry **`D-CONN04-FULL-D-IA-S23-B-S3-CLOSE`** exists.
- Current Stage 3 status is formally closed.
- Carry-forward observations remain recorded.
- Stage 4 remains explicitly unauthorized.
- Register narrative aligns with the Stage 3 closure act (no contradictory authorization found).

### Hash-seal finding (non-decision)

Embedded SHA-256 seals for Stage 2/3 artifacts and many earlier package documents **do not equal** post-Prettier committed digests. That is an integrity-metadata limitation, not a detected change to decision IDs, verdicts, or authorization boundaries. This completeness pass accounts for **all 328** seal-like occurrences; it does not alter governance outcomes.

---

## 13. Known limitations

1. **No pre-hook parent** for 80 of 87 paths (including four critical Stage 2/3 governance docs). Pre-hook digests cannot be reconstructed from Git; only historical citations remain.
2. Prettier may have altered Markdown beyond whitespace (wrapping, table alignment). Content review found governance semantics intact; this document does not claim byte-identical preservation of pre-hook text.
3. When committed digest ≠ recorded seal, Git alone often **cannot** distinguish Prettier-only reformatting from a later substantive edit—especially for NEW files.
4. Some integrity references remain **UNRESOLVED** where path binding is ambiguous or absent; targets are **not** inferred from hash equality alone.
5. Outside-package targets (e.g. slice planning package) are hashed from current `HEAD` when readable; they are not part of the 87-path commit set.
6. This reconciliation file itself is **uncommitted** until the Project Owner authorizes a follow-up commit.

---

## 14. Non-amendment and non-authorization statements

1. **No previous decision or authorization has been amended** by this document.
2. **Stage 3 remains formally CLOSED** under **`D-CONN04-FULL-D-IA-S23-B-S3-CLOSE`**.
3. **Stage 4 and all subsequent unauthorized stages remain NOT AUTHORIZED**.
4. This reconciliation **does not authorize** implementation, deployment, production activation, or live-capital activity.
5. Historical hash values inside prior documents were **not replaced**.

---

## 15. Final integrity verdict

**RECONCILED — PASS WITH OBSERVATIONS (completeness closed)**

- Substantive governance integrity (decision IDs, IR verdicts, Stage 3 closure, Stage 4+ exclusions) is preserved in the governing commit.
- Automatic Prettier rewrote committed bytes; most embedded SHA-256 seals are **historical / recording-time** and must not be used as current seals.
- Authoritative current digests for the 87 committed artifacts are published in **Appendix A** (unchanged from the prior reviewed edition).
- Occurrence accounting now partitions all **328** seal-like unique `(source, line, hex)` candidates into mutually exclusive categories that sum to **328**.
- Observation: until a Project Owner-authorized follow-up updates or supersedes seal tables, consumers must use Appendix A (or equivalent `git cat-file` digests) for current verification.
